# Type-hint audit (2026-10-21)

Scope: `harness_designer/` only (799 `.py` files, 8743 functions and methods; the four Cython files `*.pyx` are not covered). File paths are relative to the repo root. Line numbers are the `def` line in the real tree.

**To use:** change `[ ]` to `[x]` in the *Fix?* column for the rows you want fixed, and write anything you want to remember in *Comments*. Nothing in the code was changed.

> **Update (2026-10-22):** the row for `harness_designer/check_types.py` (line 248, `do.<locals>._wrapper`) is stale -- that file was rewritten as the runtime type recorder and is intentionally unannotated apart from the signature of `do`, so ignore it. The explanation below about the bare-`Callable` decorator is now history: `do` and `debug.logfunc` carry a `Callable[_P, _R]` signature, so mypy, basedpyright and pyrefly all see through them and the decorator no longer has to be blanked for a fresh run. The other scratch normalisations (`tuple[X]`, `list[a, b]`, `*args: tuple[Any]`) are still needed, because the checkers misread those conventions. Line numbers in the table were refreshed on 2026-10-22, after the decorator was added to 751 more functions.

## How the findings were produced

- Every function/method was inventoried from the AST and checked against the MEMORY.md typing rules (missing hints, `Optional`, quoting, `Union` misuse, `*args`/`**kwargs` convention, `None` defaults).
- Call sites: a mypy plugin recorded every resolved call into a `harness_designer` function (25731 call sites) with the inferred type of each argument and asked mypy which parts of each argument the declared hint rejects. basedpyright and pyrefly were run on the same tree as independent cross-checks.
- Return types: mypy printed the inferred type of every `return` expression (5362), compared with each hint; pyrefly was used for generator `yield` types.
- `@_check_types.do` was blanked and `tuple[X]` / `list[a, b]` were rewritten to PEP 484 equivalents in a *scratch copy only*, because the bare-`Callable` return type of `check_types.do` makes every type checker treat ~7,800 decorated functions as untyped.
- **Confidence tags in *Why*:** `definite` = a hint contradicts the code or a call; `probable` = the checkers agree but the cause could be a caller that needs narrowing; `review` = the evidence is weak or one-sided, no reliable correction could be derived.

## Known blind spots

- Calls whose receiver type the checkers cannot infer (untyped attributes, `getattr`, callbacks, Qt slots, `CallAfter(fn, *args)` forwarding) are not checked at all.
- `None` findings marked `probable` may be unreachable at runtime (guarded by an invariant the checkers cannot see); a runtime recorder would settle those.
- Proposed class names use the file's existing `_module` aliases where present; where a new import is needed it is listed under *also needs*.

## Summary

| Finding | Functions |
|---|---|
| A call passes a type the parameter hint rejects | 137 |
| A call can pass `None` to a parameter whose hint excludes it | 129 |
| A call passes a value statically typed as a supertype of the hint | 55 |
| A call passes a wide union (caller should narrow; no hint change) | 26 |
| A property setter is assigned a type its value hint rejects | 16 |
| Return value type is outside the return hint | 87 |
| Function returns a literal `None` but the hint excludes `None` | 14 |
| `None` can come back through an Optional attribute/call | 152 |
| A code path ends without `return` but the hint excludes `None` | 56 |
| Hint is `-> None` but the function returns a value | 2 |
| Hint promises a value but the function never returns one | 4 |
| Generator yields a type the declared yield hint rejects | 74 |
| Parameter has no type hint | 102 |
| Function has no return type hint | 32 |
| Default is `None` but the hint excludes it | 5 |
| `Union[...]` used where `X | Y` is required | 16 |
| A whole `X | None` expression is quoted | 2 |
| `**kwargs` not annotated `dict[str, Any]` | 10 |
| `*args` not annotated `tuple[Any]` | 2 |
| Annotation text differs from the MEMORY.md style | 2 |
| **Rows in the main list** | **866** |
| of which `definite` / `probable` / `review` | 265 / 523 / 78 |
| Override signatures that disagree with their base (section at the end) | 322 |
